review_id: 439
entity_slug: the-undertow-orange-beach
google_review_name: places/ChIJz4UsaEIGmogR50ckasCNrjE/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2sxR1lXdHNOSGRSVG5aaGJUTTNia3hNY2tjelJGRRAB
rating: 1
review_text: If you can’t stand smoke, then this is not the place for you. I’m mildly bothered by it but was willing to give this place a shot. The much bigger issue was the lack of any sort of customer service upon walking in. The only two employees that I saw were a bartender (who was busy) and a dj who was doing his thing. I stood waiting not knowing if I was suppose to seat myself or what. Admittedly, I wasn’t there for the drinks, but I was tempted by the AYCE shrimp.

After a few awkward minutes, I sat down at an empty table and waited. And waited. If I saw anyone to ask what to do, I probably would have, but I just waited and my bewilderment continued. Finally, after what felt like several minutes, I just got up and left.

I immediately called the establishment, and, to the female’s credit, she apologized. Apparently, I was suppose to go up to the bar and order? If there were any signs, I sure didn’t see them. She said that they’d make it right if I came back, but I was already back to Gulf Shores by then.

I can’t comment on the food. Maybe it was great? But the customer service, other than the nice female on the phone, was extremely lacking.

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: 6af813e7-81c4-45a7-8a93-c8110406888d
entity_slug: the-undertow-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Josh Walters
rating: 1
body: If you can’t stand smoke, then this is not the place for you. I’m mildly bothered by it but was willing to give this place a shot. The much bigger issue was the lack of any sort of customer service upon walking in. The only two employees that I saw were a bartender (who was busy) and a dj who was doing his thing. I stood waiting not knowing if I was suppose to seat myself or what. Admittedly, I wasn’t there for the drinks, but I was tempted by the AYCE shrimp.

After a few awkward minutes, I sat down at an empty table and waited. And waited. If I saw anyone to ask what to do, I probably would have, but I just waited and my bewilderment continued. Finally, after what felt like several minutes, I just got up and left.

I immediately called the establishment, and, to the female’s credit, she apologized. Apparently, I was suppose to go up to the bar and order? If there were any signs, I sure didn’t see them. She said that they’d make it right if I came back, but I was already back to Gulf Shores by then.

I can’t comment on the food. Maybe it was great? But the customer service, other than the nice female on the phone, was extremely lacking.
